to explain things, I start with the rent, then the marina, then the choir robes and the wires in the walls, and people look at me like I skipped half the story.\n- key: S022\n  name: Disorganized_Abnormal_Behavior\n  description: A state where behavior and motor control are severely disturbed, appearing as movements or reactions inappropriate to the situation.\n  manifestation: At the senior-center workshop I kept lining up the screws by size and tapping the table instead of fixing the shelf, and when the lady asked me to sit down I just stood there holding the screwdriver like I couldn't switch gears.\n- key: S024\n  name: Depressed_Mood\n  description: A state where a sad, empty, or hopeless mood persists.\n  manifestation: Most mornings I wake up with this heavy, hollow feeling, like the day already went bad before I even get my feet on the floor.\n- key: S025\n  name: Loss_of_Interest\n  description: A state where interest or pleasure in previously enjoyed activities is markedly diminished.\n  manifestation: I used to look forward to choir practice, bridge, cooking beans on Sunday, and doing little repair jobs for folks, but now I mostly let the phone ring and tell myself there is no point going.\n- key: S026\n  name: Weight_Appetite_Change\n  description: Significant increase or decrease in appetite or weight.\n  manifestation: I forget to eat until late in the day, and my pants have been hanging loose because coffee and a piece of toast feel like all I can manage.\n- key: S028\n  name: Psychomotor_Changes\n  description: Observable increase or decrease in psychomotor activity.\n  manifestation: Some days I move so slow getting dressed or walking to the mailbox that old neighbors would not know me, and other days I pace my little appointment cards pile up because I read the same line again and again and still can't decide what needs doing first.\n- key: S032\n  name: Death_Suicide\n  description: Recurrent thoughts of death, recurrent suicidal ideation, or a suicide attempt.\n  manifestation: I don't have a plan to hurt myself, but I do catch myself thinking it would be all right if I just didn't wake up and had nothing I've fallen behind on my bills, and... well, I've missed a few appointments, too. I even stopped showing up for church and the volunteer commitments I had. I just can't seem to keep my days organized anymore. Plus, I just don't trust ordinary interactions with people right now, so it's easier not to go out and do those things.\n\nDoctor: Before this began, did you tend to prefer being alone and keep emotionally distant from other people?\n\nPatient: No, I wouldn't say that. I mean, I know I just said I've always been a private person, but I didn't shut people out completely. I had friends, and I liked talking to folks at church and things like that. I wasn't cold or distant... I cared about the people in my life. It's really just been recently that I've wanted to be completely alone.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard a voice when no one else was around?\n\nPatient: No, like I said a minute ago, I don't hear any voices. I've never heard anyone talking to me when I'm by myself. It's just the stuff I see people doing, not voices.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any times when your thoughts or speech have felt very disorganized or hard for other people to follow?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I have noticed that. It seems like when I try to explain things to folks lately, they get confused. I'll start off talking about the rent, and then I'll mention the marina, then the choir robes and the wires in the walls. People just look at me like I skipped half the story. It makes sense to me when I'm saying it, but I guess they can't follow how I got from one thing to the next.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard voices that other people could not hear?\n\nPatient: No, like I was just saying a minute ago, I don't hear any voices. Nobody is talking or whispering to me that other folks can't hear. Like I said, it's just the things I see going on, but I'm not hearing things.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed yourself becoming much less motivated or less able to feel pleasure in things you used to enjoy?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I have. I used to really look forward to things, you know? Like going to choir practice, playing bridge, cooking up a pot of beans on Sundays, or even just doing little repair jobs for folks. But lately... I just don't care about any of it. I mostly just let the phone ring when people call. I just tell myself there is no point in going or doing anything anymore.\n\nDoctor: When you talk with other people now, do they often have trouble understanding what you mean because your speech gets hard to follow?\n\nPatient: Well, like I was saying a few minutes ago... yeah, they do. I guess I already mentioned that, but folks definitely get confused when I talk lately. I try to explain something and they just look at me like they lost the thread of the whole conversation. So, yeah, they do have trouble following me.\n\nDoctor: About how long have you been having the trouble with your thinking and speech becoming hard for other people to follow?\n\nPatient: I'm not really sure, to be honest. It's hard to put an exact timeline on it. I just know it's been happening for a while now, where people just can't seem to keep up with what I'm trying to say.\n\nDoctor: Have your beliefs ever been about something other than people watching or targeting you, or have they stayed focused on this same issue the whole time?\n\nPatient: No, it's pretty much stayed on that same issue the whole time. That's actually why I came in... I just know the people at my apartment are watching my comings and goings. They keep slipping notes about me to the office. Everybody keeps telling me it's just regular notices they tape on the doors, but I know what they're doing. So, yeah, it's really just been focused on that.\n\nDoctor: Before this started, were you able to work and manage your daily life about as well as most people your age?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I used to manage just fine, same as anybody else. But since this started... well, things have really gone downhill. I've fallen way behind on my bills, and I keep missing my appointments. I even stopped showing up for church and the volunteer work I used to do. I just can't seem to keep my days organized anymore. And honestly, I just don't trust ordinary interactions with people right now, so it's hard to keep up with any of it.\n\nDoctor: Have you believed that any outside force or group is controlling your thoughts or actions?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. It's just the people watching me at the apartment. I haven't felt like anyone is actually controlling my thoughts or anything like that. Just... keeping an eye on me.\n\nDoctor: Have you also believed that the people at your apartment are controlling or putting thoughts into your mind?\n\nPatient: No, no, nothing like that. Like I said, I don't feel like anyone's putting thoughts in my head or controlling what I do. They're just watching me, keeping tabs on me and trying to get me in trouble with the office. My thoughts are my own.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. It makes sense to me when I'm saying it, but I guess they can't follow how I got from one thing to the next.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard voices that other people could not hear?\n\nPatient: No, like I was just saying a minute ago, I don't hear any voices. Nobody is talking or whispering to me that other folks can't hear. Like I said, it's just the things I see going on, but I'm not hearing things.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed yourself becoming much less motivated or less able to feel pleasure in things you used to enjoy?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I have. I used to really look forward to things, you know? Like going to choir practice, playing bridge, cooking up a pot of beans on Sundays, or even just doing little repair jobs for folks. But lately... I just don't care about any of it. I mostly just let the phone ring when people call. I just tell myself there is no point in going or doing anything anymore.\n\nDoctor: When you talk with other people now, do they often have trouble understanding what you mean because your speech gets hard to follow?\n\nPatient: Well, like I was saying a few minutes ago... yeah, they do. I guess I already mentioned that, but folks definitely get confused when I talk lately. I try to explain something and they just look at me like they lost the thread of the whole conversation. So, yeah, they do have trouble following me.\n\nDoctor: About how long have you been having the trouble with your thinking and speech becoming hard for other people to follow?\n\nPatient: I'm not really sure, to be honest. It's hard to put an exact timeline on it. I just know it's been happening for a while now, where people just can't seem to keep up with what I'm trying to say.\n\nDoctor: Have your beliefs ever been about something other than people watching or targeting you, or have they stayed focused on this same issue the whole time?\n\nPatient: No, it's pretty much stayed on that same issue the whole time. That's actually why I came in... I just know the people at my apartment are watching my comings and goings. They keep slipping notes about me to the office. Everybody keeps telling me it's just regular notices they tape on the doors, but I know what they're doing. So, yeah, it's really just been focused on that.\n\nDoctor: Before this started, were you able to work and manage your daily life about as well as most people your age?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I used to manage just fine, same as anybody else. But since this started... well, things have really gone downhill. I've fallen way behind on my bills, and I keep missing my appointments. I even stopped showing up for church and the volunteer work I used to do. I just can't seem to keep my days organized anymore. And honestly, I just don't trust ordinary interactions with people right now, so it's hard to keep up with any of it.\n\nDoctor: Have you believed that any outside force or group is controlling your thoughts or actions?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. It's just the people watching me at the apartment. I haven't felt like anyone is actually controlling my thoughts or anything like that. Just... keeping an eye on me.\n\nDoctor: Have you also believed that the people at your apartment are controlling or putting thoughts into your mind?\n\nPatient: No, no, nothing like that. Like I said, I don't feel like anyone's putting thoughts in my head or controlling what I do. They're just watching me, keeping tabs on me and trying to get me in trouble with the office. My thoughts are my own.\n\nDoctor: Have these beliefs and the trouble with your thinking made it harder for you to do your usual work or daily tasks than you were able to before?\n\nPatient: Yeah, it really has. Like I mentioned before, I've fallen way behind on my bills and I keep missing my appointments. I just can't seem to keep my days organized anymore. And I've stopped going to church and doing my volunteer work. It's just... I don't trust ordinary interactions with people right now, with everything going on. So it's made doing my usual stuff a lot harder than it used to be.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. Never bundle several symptoms, or several attributes of one symptom, into a single question — e.g. asking whether a symptom is present and how long it has lasted at the same time.\n- If you need to ask about more than one thing, ask the one that tells you the most now and come back to the rest later.\n- Keep the question clear enough for the patient to understand.\n- Respond in English.\n\n[Output Format — JSON only, no other text]\n{\"intent\": \"<what you want to find out with this question>\", \"question\": \"<how you actually ask it in the interview>\"}\n"

It's really just been recently that I've wanted to be completely alone.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard a voice when no one else was around?\n\nPatient: No, like I said a minute ago, I don't hear any voices. I've never heard anyone talking to me when I'm by myself. It's just the stuff I see people doing, not voices.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any times when your thoughts or speech have felt very disorganized or hard for other people to follow?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I have noticed that. It seems like when I try to explain things to folks lately, they get confused. I'll start off talking about the rent, and then I'll mention the marina, then the choir robes and the wires in the walls. People just look at me like I skipped half the story. It makes sense to me when I'm saying it, but I guess they can't follow how I got from one thing to the next.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard voices that other people could not hear?\n\nPatient: No, like I was just saying a minute ago, I don't hear any voices. Nobody is talking or whispering to me that other folks can't hear. Like I said, it's just the things I see going on, but I'm not hearing things.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed yourself becoming much less motivated or less able to feel pleasure in things you used to enjoy?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I have. I used to really look forward to things, you know? Like going to choir practice, playing bridge, cooking up a pot of beans on Sundays, or even just doing little repair jobs for folks. But lately... I just don't care about any of it. I mostly just let the phone ring when people call. I just tell myself there is no point in going or doing anything anymore.\n\nDoctor: When you talk with other people now, do they often have trouble understanding what you mean because your speech gets hard to follow?\n\nPatient: Well, like I was saying a few minutes ago... yeah, they do. I guess I already mentioned that, but folks definitely get confused when I talk lately. I try to explain something and they just look at me like they lost the thread of the whole conversation. So, yeah, they do have trouble following me.\n\nDoctor: About how long have you been having the trouble with your thinking and speech becoming hard for other people to follow?\n\nPatient: I'm not really sure, to be honest. It's hard to put an exact timeline on it. I just know it's been happening for a while now, where people just can't seem to keep up with what I'm trying to say.\n\nDoctor: Have your beliefs ever been about something other than people watching or targeting you, or have they stayed focused on this same issue the whole time?\n\nPatient: No, it's pretty much stayed on that same issue the whole time. That's actually why I came in... I just know the people at my apartment are watching my comings and goings. They keep slipping notes about me to the office. Everybody keeps telling me it's just regular notices they tape on the doors, but I know what they're doing. So, yeah, it's really just been focused on that.\n\nDoctor: Before this started, were you able to work and manage your daily life about as well as most people your age?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I used to manage just fine, same as anybody else. But since this started... well, things have really gone downhill. I've fallen way behind on my bills, and I keep missing my appointments. I even stopped showing up for church and the volunteer work I used to do. I just can't seem to keep my days organized anymore. And honestly, I just don't trust ordinary interactions with people right now, so it's hard to keep up with any of it.\n\nDoctor: Have you believed that any outside force or group is controlling your thoughts or actions?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. It's just the people watching me at the apartment. I haven't felt like anyone is actually controlling my thoughts or anything like that. Just... keeping an eye on me.\n\nDoctor: Have you also believed that the people at your apartment are controlling or putting thoughts into your mind?\n\nPatient: No, no, nothing like that. Like I said, I don't feel like anyone's putting thoughts in my head or controlling what I do. They're just watching me, keeping tabs on me and trying to get me in trouble with the office. My thoughts are my own.\n\nDoctor: Have these beliefs and the trouble with your thinking made it harder for you to do your usual work or daily tasks than you were able to before?\n\nPatient: Yeah, it really has. Like I mentioned before, I've fallen way behind on my bills and I keep missing my appointments. I just can't seem to keep my days organized anymore. And I've stopped going to church and doing my volunteer work. It's just... I don't trust ordinary interactions with people right now, with everything going on. So it's made doing my usual stuff a lot harder than it used to be.\n\nDoctor: Do other people around you share this belief that the apartment building staff and neighbors are watching and sending notes about you?\n\nPatient: No, they don't. I mean, nobody else seems to see it. When I've tried to tell my family or... or the few people I still talk to, they just tell me I'm imagining things. They don't believe me at all. They think it's all in my head.\n\nDoctor: Have these beliefs and the trouble with your thinking been present continuously for at least six months?\n\nPatient: Yes, it's been a lot longer than six months. This has been going on for about a year and five months now. It all started right after I left the house I shared with my wife and moved into this senior apartment building. That's when all this began."
